Strongbox

MNQ Strong Book (3-Leg)

MNQ · 3 legs — only legs that reproduced the reference tapes 100%
29.98×Return on drawdown · best window
Return on drawdown · what a dollar of pain buys 29.98×

RoDD is the metric this catalog is priced on. Profit factor says the engine works; RoDD says what it costs to hold: net profit divided by the worst peak-to-valley drawdown. This system’s window: $79.7k net ÷ $2,700 max drawdown = 29.98×. Slide your drawdown budget — the projection scales with it, and so does the pain.

NOT BUILT FOR THIS SIZE The published max drawdown on this window ($2,700) exceeds a $1k budget — this sizing would not have survived the sample. Some systems are not meant to run this small.
NOT BUILT FOR THIS SIZE The published max drawdown on this window ($2,700) exceeds a $2.5k budget — this sizing would not have survived the sample. Some systems are not meant to run this small.
THIN CUSHION The published drawdown is more than half this budget — one bad stretch uses most of your room. A drawdown budget of $5k historically returned ~$149,900 over this window (≈$21,414/mo average).
RECOMMENDED SIZE The smallest budget with a full cushion over the published drawdown. A drawdown budget of $10k historically returned ~$299,800 over this window (≈$42,829/mo average).
A drawdown budget of $25k historically returned ~$749,500 over this window (≈$107,071/mo average).
A drawdown budget of $50k historically returned ~$1,499,000 over this window (≈$214,143/mo average).

Linear scaling of the published window; not a projection of future results. See the disclaimer below.
